			limit = {

					exists = yes
				}
			}

				target = GXC
				type = annex_everything
			}
		}

			add_state_claim = 592

			add_state_claim = 594

shared_focus = {
	id = YUN_join_the_right_KMT_committee
	icon = GFX_goal_RKMT_A_New_China
	cost = 7.2
	prerequisite = {
		focus = YUN_Reform_KMT_Organization
	}
	mutually_exclusive = {
		focus = YUN_join_the_left_KMT_committee
	}
	available = {
		has_government = social_liberal
		is_subject = no
		is_in_faction = no
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		factor = 50
	}
	x = 28
	y = 4
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus YUN_join_the_right_KMT_committee"
		if = {
			limit = {
				GXC = {
					is_faction_leader = yes
					has_government = authoritarian_democrat
				}
			}
			GXC = { country_event = yun.518 }
			else = {
				save_global_event_target_as = yunnan_R_KMT_faction_leader

			create_faction_from_template = { 
				template = faction_template_generic_dominance              
				name = yunnan_R_KMT_faction
				icon = "GFX_faction_guomindang"
			}				
				set_potential_national_government = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						GXC = {
							exists = yes
							has_government = authoritarian_democrat
						}
					}
					GXC = { country_event = yun.522 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }
}
